| moldova-income-tax-OLD-093 | ### 5.1 The Flat 12% Rate | Who this applies to; Rates, bands and thresholds; The base; Cross-border points; Sources: Not yet confirmed. Source/currency/scope or legal rule preserved/corrected or explicitly unconfirmed; independent comparison pending |
| moldova-income-tax-OLD-094 | - **Flat 12% rate rule** — Resident individuals pay a flat 12% on employment, professional and entrepreneurial income, capital gains, and other taxable income -- there are no progressive brackets in 2025. Farming/agricultural enterprises are taxed at a reduced 7% on entrepreneurial income.  _(Tax Code, Title II [PwC Moldova Individual -- Taxes on personal income; PwC])_ | Who this applies to; Rates, bands and thresholds; The base; Cross-border points; Sources: Not yet confirmed. Source/currency/scope or legal rule preserved/corrected or explicitly unconfirmed; independent comparison pending |

| moldova-income-tax-OLD-095 | ### 5.2 The Monthly Payroll PIT Base | Who this applies to; Rates, bands and thresholds; The base; Cross-border points; Sources: Not yet confirmed. Source/currency/scope or legal rule preserved/corrected or explicitly unconfirmed; independent comparison pending |
| moldova-income-tax-OLD-096 | - **Monthly PIT base formula** — Monthly PIT base = gross salary − employee CAS (6%) − employee CNAM (9%) − applicable monthly personal/dependent exemptions. The 12% rate is applied to that base. PIT is withheld and remitted by the employer by the 25th of the following month.  _(Tax Code, Title II; administrative practice [buhgalter.md; playroll])_ | Who this applies to; Rates, bands and thresholds; The base; Cross-border points; Sources: Not yet confirmed. Source/currency/scope or legal rule preserved/corrected or explicitly unconfirmed; independent comparison pending |
| moldova-income-tax-OLD-096-L001 | - **Monthly PIT base formula** — Monthly PIT base = gross salary − employee CAS (6%) − employee CNAM (9%) − applicable monthly personal/dependent exemptions. | moldova-income-tax-OLD-120 | - **IT Park single tax** — Residents of the Moldova Innovation Technology Park pay a single 7% tax on turnover (excl. VAT), covering corporate income tax, employee PIT, social and health contributions, and most local/real-estate/road taxes -- but not less than a per-employee minimum equal to 30% of the average monthly forecast salary. Eligibility requires 70%+ of revenue from qualifying IT activities. If a client is an IT-Park resident, do NOT run the general 12% computation (Refusal R-MD-6).  _([PwC; deschidecompanie.md])_ | The base: Capital gains, property and financial receipts; The base: Professional practice and separate turnover regimes; Sources: Not yet confirmed. Final/preliminary withholding and retail/service/IT-park distinctions reconciled; full specialist gaps retained |

## Removed and why

Baseline 46135 bytes; current 90218 bytes. More than 25% shorter: False.
- Duplicated old headings/rate tables/boilerplate consolidated into the canonical nine sections; every original rule/table/bullet has a destination.
- Unsupported employee 6% social withholding and additive 27% model corrected to statutory category/base method; old fixtures archived and explicitly corrected.
- Guessed employer category, exemption, missing-IDNP refusal, non-cumulative payroll and legal-year assumptions replaced by actual facts and official rules.
- Old primary-source gaps/historical amounts are explicit, not certified as current 2026 law. No stamped CTA or attribution removed.

Independent whole-pack comparison and any lost-content restoration count are pending. No shortened rewrite can be published without the required comparison.

## Assumed calculations

The two current Guide blocks and hashes are retained in the JSON. All case facts are expressly assumed; they are not extra statutory thresholds. Predecessor numerical fixtures and bank examples remain mapped/archived. Independent legal-assumption review and recomputation are pending.

## Subsequently merged repair PR #1024

The full incoming file and all 106 units are preserved in the [repair predecessor table](md-country-evidence/public-merged-repair-predecessor-reconciliation.md). Published review notes cover the smaller incoming file; they do not approve this expanded country pack. Existing source rows, cases and old-topic destinations remain unchanged.
